yd,yp=LQ,oI local function yu(yY,yh)local yM=1 local function yF()local yk,yb=0,1 while true do local yQ=yV(yY,yM);yM=yM+1 if not yQ or yb>9007199254740992 then UI[UU(56,5,0x59)]((UU(0x3927,36,0x58)),0)end yk=yk+yQ%0x80*yb;if yQ<0x80 then return yk end;yb=yb*0x80 end end local yy if yh then yy=function()local yE=yF();if yM+yE-1>#yY then UI[UU(56,5,0x59)]((UU(0x3954,0x21,0x9F)),0)end local yq={};for ym=1,yE do yq[ym]=ya(yV(yY,yM));yM=yM+1 end return ys(yq)end end return yF,function()return yM==#yY+1 end,yy end local function yS(yX,yv)if yi(yX)~=(UU(1,6,242))or#yX<9 or yi(yv)~=(UU(0xC4,6,76))then UI[UU(0x38,5,89)]((UU(0x3981,0x25,125)),0)end yv=yv%0x7FFFFFFF local yz={}for yB=1,#yX do yv=yv*0xBC8F%0x7FFFFFFF;yz[yB]=(yV(yX,yB)-yv%0x100)%0x100 end local yZ=yz[1]%9 if yZ==0 then return nil elseif yZ==1 then return false elseif yZ==2 then return true elseif yZ==4 then return 1/0 elseif
